Estimating the Pose of a Euro Pallet with an RGB Camera based on Synthetic Training Data"><meta name="description" content="PalLoc6D contains 50 000 synthetically generated images of a photorealistic pallet in a domain-radomized environment. PalLoc6D includes annotations of the pallets' 6D pose. The data was created using the NVIDIA Dataset Synthesizer (NDDS, https://github.com/NVIDIA/Dataset_Synthesizer). Additionally, a photorealistic 3D model of a Euro pallet is provided. PalLoc6D can be used to train neural networks for RGB camera-based 6D pallet pose estimation, such as Nvidia's &quot;Deep Object Pose Estimation&quot; (DOPE, https://github.com/NVlabs/Deep_Object_Pose). Furthermore, the weights of the DOPE algorithm, trained with the annotated images are included in PalLoc6D to allow a quick start for experimenting with 6D pose estimation.

PalLoc6D was published as part of the paper &quot;Estimating the Pose of a Euro Pallet with an RGB Camera based on Synthetic Training Data&quot;, which was presented at the WGTL Fachkolloquium 2022 in Bremen and will be subsequently published in the Logistics Journal.
The purpose, creation, and validation of the dataset are further elaborated in the publication. 

Paper abstract:
&quot;Estimating the pose of a pallet and other logistics objects is crucial for various use cases, such as automatized material handling or tracking. Innovations in computer vision, computing power, and machine learning open up new opportunities for device-free localization based on cameras and neural networks. Large image datasets with annotated poses are required for training the network. Manual annotation, especially of 6D poses, is an extremely labor-intensive process. Hence, newer approaches often leverage synthetic training data to automatize the process of generating annotated image datasets. In this work, the generation of synthetic training data for 6D pose estimation of pallets is presented. The data is then used to train the Deep Object Pose Estimation (DOPE) algorithm. The experimental validation of the algorithm proves that the 6D pose estimation of a standardized Euro pallet with a Red-Green-Blue (RGB) camera is feasible. The comparison of the results from three varying datasets under different lighting conditions shows the relevance of an appropriate dataset design to achieve an accurate and robust localization. The quantitative evaluation shows an average position error of less than 20 cm for the preferred dataset. The validated training dataset and a photorealistic model of a Euro pallet are publicly provided.&quot;"><meta name="og:title" content="PalLoc6D - Estimating the Pose of a Euro Pallet with an RGB Camera based on Synthetic Training Data"><meta name="og:description" content="PalLoc6D contains 50 000 synthetically generated images of a photorealistic pallet in a domain-radomized environment. The purpose, creation, and validation of the dataset are further elaborated in the publication.

class="ng-tns-c755-1">Adamanov, Asan</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1"></td></tr><tr _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1 ng-star-inserted"><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">dc.contributor.author</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">Hinckeldeyn, Johannes</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1"></td></tr><tr _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1 ng-star-inserted"><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">dc.contributor.author</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">Kreutzfeldt, Jochen</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1"></td></tr><!----><!----><!----><tr _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1 ng-star-inserted"><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">dc.date.accessioned</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">2022-08-26T10:26:21Z</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1"></td></tr><!----><!----><!----><tr _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1 ng-star-inserted"><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">dc.date.available</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">2022-08-26T10:26:21Z</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1"></td></tr><!----><!----><!----><tr _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1 ng-star-inserted"><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">dc.date.issued</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">2022-08-26</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1"></td></tr><!----><!----><!----><tr _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1 ng-star-inserted"><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">dc.description.abstract</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">PalLoc6D contains 50 000 synthetically generated images of a photorealistic pallet in a domain-radomized environment. PalLoc6D includes annotations of the pallets' 6D pose. The data was created using the NVIDIA Dataset Synthesizer (NDDS, https://github.com/NVIDIA/Dataset_Synthesizer). Additionally, a photorealistic 3D model of a Euro pallet is provided. PalLoc6D can be used to train neural networks for RGB camera-based 6D pallet pose estimation, such as Nvidia's "Deep Object Pose Estimation" (DOPE, https://github.com/NVlabs/Deep_Object_Pose). Furthermore, the weights of the DOPE algorithm, trained with the annotated images are included in PalLoc6D to allow a quick start for experimenting with 6D pose estimation.

PalLoc6D was published as part of the paper "Estimating the Pose of a Euro Pallet with an RGB Camera based on Synthetic Training Data", which was presented at the WGTL Fachkolloquium 2022 in Bremen and will be subsequently published in the Logistics Journal.
The purpose, creation, and validation of the dataset are further elaborated in the publication. 

Paper abstract:
"Estimating the pose of a pallet and other logistics objects is crucial for various use cases, such as automatized material handling or tracking. Innovations in computer vision, computing power, and machine learning open up new opportunities for device-free localization based on cameras and neural networks. Large image datasets with annotated poses are required for training the network. Manual annotation, especially of 6D poses, is an extremely labor-intensive process. Hence, newer approaches often leverage synthetic training data to automatize the process of generating annotated image datasets. In this work, the generation of synthetic training data for 6D pose estimation of pallets is presented. The data is then used to train the Deep Object Pose Estimation (DOPE) algorithm. The experimental validation of the algorithm proves that the 6D pose estimation of a standardized Euro pallet with a Red-Green-Blue (RGB) camera is feasible. The comparison of the results from three varying datasets under different lighting conditions shows the relevance of an appropriate dataset design to achieve an accurate and robust localization. The quantitative evaluation shows an average position error of less than 20 cm for the preferred dataset. The validated training dataset and a photorealistic model of a Euro pallet are publicly provided."</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">en</td></tr><!----><!----><!----><tr _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1 ng-star-inserted"><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">dc.identifier.doi</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1">10.15480/336.4470</td><td _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1"></td></tr><!----><!----><!----><tr _ngcontent-sc755="" class="ng-tns-c755-1 ng-star-inserted"><td _ngcontent-sc755="" alt=\&q;\&q; style=\&q;max-width: 1em;max-height:1em;margin-left: .2em;\&q;&g;&q;,&q;place&q;:0,&q;authority&q;:null,&q;confidence&q;:-1}],&q;dc.rights.license&q;:[{&q;uuid&q;:&q;501ed4fd-dcc1-4c30-9b7f-749ff13e94bf&q;,&q;language&q;:null,&q;value&q;:&q;&l;h1&g;Veröffentlichungslizenz für Forschungsdaten auf “TUHH Open Research” der Technischen Universität Hamburg (TUHH)&l;/h1&g;\r\n\r\nDie Universitätsbibliothek betreibt für die TUHH die Plattform “TUHH Open Research”.\r\n\r\n&l;strong&g;Mit den folgenden Bestimmungen für die Veröffentlichung von Forschungsdaten auf “TUHH Open Research” bin ich einverstanden:&l;/strong&g;\r\n\r\nRechteübertragung\r\n&l;ol&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Ich übertrage hiermit der Universitätsbibliothek das einfache, zeitlich unbeschränkte Recht, die hochgeladenen Daten (Forschungsdaten mit den zugehörigen Metadaten) elektronisch zu speichern.&l;/li&g;\r\n&l;/ol&g;\r\nLangzeitarchivierung und Speicherung\r\n&l;ol start=\&q;2\&q;&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Die Universitätsbibliothek ist zum Zwecke der Langzeitarchivierung berechtigt, meine abgelieferten Daten in ein anderes Datenformat zu konvertieren. Die Universitätsbibliothek behält sich das Recht vor, die Daten auf einem anderen Server zu hinterlegen.&l;/li&g;\r\n&l;/ol&g;\r\nLizenzbestimmungen\r\n&l;ol start=\&q;3\&q;&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Metadaten (beschreibende Daten) sind im Internet öffentlich zugänglich. Sie werden inkl. Abstract unter der Lizenz Creative Commons Zero (CC0, vgl. &l;a href=\&q;https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/\&q; target=\&q;_blank\&q; rel=\&q;noopener\&q;&g;https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/&l;/a&g;) veröffentlicht, d.h. diese Daten können ohne Einschränkungen weitergegeben und weiterverwendet werden.&l;/li&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Forschungsdaten (Dateien) können, wenn notwendig, mit einem Zugriffsschutz oder einer Embargofrist versehen werden. In diesen Fällen ist eine Weiterverteilung ohne meine Zustimmung ausgeschlossen. Forschungdaten sollten idealerweise unter einer offenen Lizenz stehen, um die Nachnutzbarkeit zu ermöglichen.\r\nForschungsdaten können nach Ablauf von Aufbewahrungsfristen bei Bedarf gelöscht werden. Im Sinne der Guten Wissenschaftlichen Praxis ist eine Aufbewahrung von zehn Jahren garantiert. Die Metadaten bleiben auch nach einer eventuellen Löschung der Forschungsdaten dauerhaft über ihren persistenten Identifier verfügbar.&l;/li&g;\r\n&l;/ol&g;\r\nRechtsübergang\r\n&l;ol start=\&q;5\&q;&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Im Falle eines Überganges (des Dienstes) auf eine andere Institution erkläre ich mich damit einverstanden, dass die eingeräumten Rechte in gleicher Weise von der neuen Institution fortgeführt und genutzt werden können. Einer gesonderten Zustimmung bedarf es in diesem Falle nicht.&l;/li&g;\r\n&l;/ol&g;\r\n&a;nbsp;\r\n\r\n&l;strong&g;Folgende Bestimmungen zum Urheber- und Persönlichkeitsrecht gelten:&l;/strong&g;\r\n\r\nRechte Dritter und Haftungsfreistellung\r\n&l;ol&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Ich versichere, dass mit der Veröffentlichung dieser Daten keine Rechte, insbesondere Urheber- und Persönlichkeitsrechte Dritter, verletzt werden. Sollte ich später Kenntnis vom Bestehen oder der Entstehung von Rechtshindernissen erhalten, setze ich die Universitätsbibliothek unverzüglich in Kenntnis. Von etwaigen Ansprüchen Dritter stelle ich die TUHH frei.&l;/li&g;\r\n&l;/ol&g;\r\nMehrere Beteiligte\r\n&l;ol start=\&q;2\&q;&g;\r\n \t&l;li&g;Sind mehrere Urheber an der Forschungsdatenpublikation beteiligt, bin ich zur Veröffentlichung berechtigt. Werden Forschungsdaten im Auftrag vom Urheber hochgeladen, versichere ich hiermit, dass ich dazu berechtigt Jochen&q;,&q;place&q;:4,&q;authority&q;:&q;d6fbdc42-0742-4a5f-93a0-3486f85934fe&q;,&q;confidence&q;:600}],&q;dc.date.accessioned&q;:[{&q;uuid&q;:&q;6d2a4ab7-3ce3-4aa3-a927-c1c6c3a1e665&q;,&q;language&q;:null,&q;value&q;:&q;2022-08-26T10:26:21Z&q;,&q;place&q;:0,&q;authority&q;:null,&q;confidence&q;:-1}],&q;dc.date.available&q;:[{&q;uuid&q;:&q;bb76ed19-1646-4e84-b203-69ce2c49168a&q;,&q;language&q;:null,&q;value&q;:&q;2022-08-26T10:26:21Z&q;,&q;place&q;:0,&q;authority&q;:null,&q;confidence&q;:-1}],&q;dc.date.issued&q;:[{&q;uuid&q;:&q;1b539415-7775-4b55-b366-ba862691f65c&q;,&q;language&q;:null,&q;value&q;:&q;2022-08-26&q;,&q;place&q;:0,&q;authority&q;:null,&q;confidence&q;:-1}],&q;dc.description.abstract&q;:[{&q;uuid&q;:&q;32d56dbe-8b54-44fc-a6e3-936e876088ff&q;,&q;language&q;:&q;en&q;,&q;value&q;:&q;PalLoc6D contains 50 000 synthetically generated images of a photorealistic pallet in a domain-radomized environment. PalLoc6D includes annotations of the pallets&s; 6D pose. The data was created using the NVIDIA Dataset Synthesizer (NDDS, https://github.com/NVIDIA/Dataset_Synthesizer). Additionally, a photorealistic 3D model of a Euro pallet is provided. PalLoc6D can be used to train neural networks for RGB camera-based 6D pallet pose estimation, such as Nvidia&s;s \&q;Deep Object Pose Estimation\&q; (DOPE, https://github.com/NVlabs/Deep_Object_Pose). Furthermore, the weights of the DOPE algorithm, trained with the annotated images are included in PalLoc6D to allow a quick start for experimenting with 6D pose estimation.\r\n\r\nPalLoc6D was published as part of the paper \&q;Estimating the Pose of a Euro Pallet with an RGB Camera based on Synthetic Training Data\&q;, which was presented at the WGTL Fachkolloquium 2022 in Bremen and will be subsequently published in the Logistics Journal.\r\nThe purpose, creation, and validation of the dataset are further elaborated in the publication. \r\n\r\nPaper abstract:\r\n\&q;Estimating the pose of a pallet and other logistics objects is crucial for various use cases, such as automatized material handling or tracking. Innovations in computer vision, computing power, and machine learning open up new opportunities for device-free localization based on cameras and neural networks. Large image datasets with annotated poses are required for training the network. Manual annotation, especially of 6D poses, is an extremely labor-intensive process. Hence, newer approaches often leverage synthetic training data to automatize the process of generating annotated image datasets. In this work, the generation of synthetic training data for 6D pose estimation of pallets is presented. The data is then used to train the Deep Object Pose Estimation (DOPE) algorithm. The experimental validation of the algorithm proves that the 6D pose estimation of a standardized Euro pallet with a Red-Green-Blue (RGB) camera is feasible. The comparison of the results from three varying datasets under different lighting conditions shows the relevance of an appropriate dataset design to achieve an accurate and robust localization. The quantitative evaluation shows an average position error of less than 20 cm for the preferred dataset. The validated training dataset and a photorealistic model of a Euro pallet are publicly
